ENTRY 175: Interpretation limits for authorized continuation
======================================================================
Only decoded instructions at verified entry boundaries are used as native proof. Decompiler cursor 300 continues the initial actor-body page rather than a second independent decompilation. Fixed instruction counts may show subsequent functions; only the named function through its return is used. Point-light +0xB8 method semantics are verified from the actual body and fallback writer, not guessed from method labels. NiAVObject +0x18 is the parent input and +0x1C dispatches another object: do not mislabel +0x1C as parent. The analyzed factory xref at 0x004F883D names a different target (0x00A7D510); exclude it as point-light factory caller evidence. No all-writer or all-thread closure is implied by these xrefs.
